Determine which blister card count to use for 56-day fills.\n        # Primary interpretation: a 56-day fill uses a card with double the blister count.\n        # If that exact size is not present in card_cost.csv, fall back to the medication's\n        # existing blister_card_count (data-driven conservative default).\n        blister_count_56 = blister_count_28 * 2\n        if blister_count_56 not in card_cost_by_count:\n            blister_count_56 = blister_count_28\n\n        if blister_count_28 not in card_cost_by_count:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"No card_cost_usd found for blister_card_count={blister_count_28} (medication={med}).\"\n            )\n        if blister_count_56 not in card_cost_by_count:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"No card_cost_usd found for blister_card_count={blister_count_56} (medication={med}).\"\n            )\n\n        card_cost_28 = card_cost_by_count[blister_count_28]\n        card_cost_56 = card_cost_by_count[blister_count_56]\n\n        reimbursement_per_cycle = reimbursement_by_med[med]\n\n        # Drug costs.\n        annual_drug_cost_28 = (price_per_1000 / Decimal(\"1000\")) * capsules_28 * fills_28 * patients\n        annual_drug_cost_56 = (price_per_1000 / Decimal(\"1000\")) * capsules_56 * fills_56 * patients\n\n        # Packaging costs.\n        annual_pack_cost_28 = card_cost_28 * patients * fills_28\n        annual_pack_cost_56 = card_cost_56 * patients * fills_56\n\n        # Reimbursement.\n        annual_reimb_28 = reimbursement_per_cycle * fills_28\n        annual_reimb_56 = reimbursement_per_cycle * fills_56\n\n        # Margins.\n        annual_margin_28 = annual_reimb_28 - annual_drug_cost_28 - annual_pack_cost_28\n        annual_margin_56 = annual_reimb_56 - annual_drug_cost_56 - annual_pack_cost_56\n        diff_56_minus_28 = annual_margin_56 - annual_margin_28\n\n        medications.append(\n            {\n                \"medication\": med,\n                \"price_per_1000_capsules_usd\": money_f(price_per_1000),\n                \"blister_card_count_28_day\": blister_count_28,\n                \"blister_card_count_56_day\": blister_count_56,\n                \"card_cost_28_day_usd\": money_f(card_cost_28),\n                \"card_cost_56_day_usd\": money_f(card_cost_56),\n                \"reimbursement_per_cycle_180_patients_usd\": money_f(reimbursement_per_cycle),\n                \"annual_drug_cost_28_day_usd\": money_f(annual_drug_cost_28),\n                \"annual_drug_cost_56_day_usd\": money_f(annual_drug_cost_56),\n                \"annual_packaging_cost_28_day_usd\": money_f(annual_pack_cost_28),\n                \"annual_packaging_cost_56_day_usd\": money_f(annual_pack_cost_56),\n                \"annual_reimbursement_28_day_usd\": money_f(annual_reimb_28),\n                \"annual_reimbursement_56_day_usd\": money_f(annual_reimb_56),\n                \"annual_margin_28_day_usd\": money_f(annual_margin_28),\n                \"annual_margin_56_day_usd\": money_f(annual_margin_56),\n                \"annual_margin_difference_56_minus_28_usd\": money_f(diff_56_minus_28),\n            }\n        )\n\n    total_margin_28 = sum((Decimal(str(m[\"annual_margin_28_day_usd\"])) for m in medications), Decimal(\"0\"))\n    total_margin_56 = sum((Decimal(str(m[\"annual_margin_56_day_usd\"])) for m in medications), Decimal(\"0\"))\n    total_diff = sum(\n        (Decimal(str(m[\"annual_margin_difference_56_minus_28_usd\"])) for m in medications),\n        Decimal(\"0\"),\n    )\n\n    # Re-round totals to cents.\n    total_margin_28 = money(total_margin_28)\n    total_margin_56 = money(total_margin_56)\n    total_diff = money(total_diff)\n    abs_total_diff = money(abs(total_diff))\n\n    threshold = Decimal(\"9000\")\n    decision = \"convert_to_56_day\" if abs_total_diff < threshold else \"keep_28_day\"\n\n    analysis = {\n        \"medications\": medications,\n        \"totals\": {\n            \"total_annual_margin_28_day_usd\": money_f(total_margin_28),\n            \"total_annual_margin_56_day_usd\": money_f(total_margin_56),\n            \"total_annual_margin_difference_56_minus_28_usd\": money_f(total_diff),\n            \"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": money_f(abs_total_diff),\n        },\n        \"recommendation\": {\n            \"decision\": decision,\n            \"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": money_f(abs_total_diff),\n            \"threshold_usd\": money_f(threshold),\n        },\n    }\n\n    OUT_JSON.write_text(json.dumps(analysis, indent=2, sort_keys=False) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n    summary_lines = [\n        f\"Total 28-day margin (USD): {money_f(total_margin_28):.2f}\",\n        f\"Total 56-day margin (USD): {money_f(total_margin_56):.2f}\",\n        f\"Absolute difference (USD): {money_f(abs_total_diff):.2f}\",\n        f\"Decision: {decision}\",\n    ]\n    OUT_MD.write_text(\"\\n\".join(summary_lines) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n    # Parse numeric CSV fields robustly.\n    # Keep as Decimal to avoid float rounding drift.\n    return Decimal(str(s).strip())\n\n\ndef money(x: Decimal) -> Decimal:\n    return x.quantize(TWOPLACES, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)\n\n\ndef money_f(x: Decimal) -> float:\n    return float(money(x))\n\n\ndef read_csv(path: Path):\n    with path.open(newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        rows = list(reader)\n    if not rows:\n        raise ValueError(f\"CSV had no data rows: {path}\")\n    return rows\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    ingredient_rows = read_csv(INGREDIENT_COST_CSV)\n    card_rows = read_csv(CARD_COST_CSV)\n    reimbursement_rows = read_csv(REIMBURSEMENT_CSV)\n\n    # Card costs keyed by blister_card_count.\n    card_cost_by_count = {}\n    for r in card_rows:\n        try:\n            c = int(str(r[\"blister_card_count\"]).strip())\n        except Exception as e:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Invalid blister_card_count in {CARD_COST_CSV}: {r}\") from e\n        card_cost_by_count[c] = d(r[\"card_cost_usd\"])\n\n    ingredient_by_med = {}\n    for r in ingredient_rows:\n        med = str(r[\"medication\"]).strip()\n        ingredient_by_med[med] = {\n            \"price_per_1000_capsules_usd\": d(r[\"price_per_1000_capsules_usd\"]),\n            \"blister_card_count\": int(str(r[\"blister_card_count\"]).strip()),\n        }\n\n    reimbursement_by_med = {}\n    for r in reimbursement_rows:\n        med = str(r[\"medication\"]).strip()\n        reimbursement_by_med[med] = d(r[\"reimbursement_per_cycle_180_patients_usd\"])\n\n    meds_ingredient = set(ingredient_by_med.keys())\n    meds_reimb = set(reimbursement_by_med.keys())\n    if meds_ingredient != meds_reimb:\n        missing_in_reimb = sorted(meds_ingredient - meds_reimb)\n        missing_in_ing = sorted(meds_reimb - meds_ingredient)\n        raise ValueError(\n            \"Medication mismatch between ingredient_cost.csv and reimbursement.csv. \"\n            f\"Missing in reimbursement: {missing_in_reimb}. Missing in ingredient: {missing_in_ing}.\"\n        )\n\n    medications = []\n\n    # Constants from the task.\n    patients = Decimal(\"180\")\n    capsules_28 = Decimal(\"56\")\n    fills_28 = Decimal(\"12\")\n    capsules_56 = Decimal(\"112\")\n    fills_56 = Decimal(\"6\")\n\n    # Annual capsules across all patients should match between 28/56 day models.\n    # 56*12 == 112*6 == 672 capsules/patient/year.\n    for med in sorted(meds_ingredient):\n        ing = ingredient_by_med[med]\n        price_per_1000 = ing[\"price_per_1000_capsules_usd\"]\n        blister_count_28 = int(ing[\"blister_card_count\"])\n\n        # Determine which blister card count to use for 56-day fills.\n        # Primary interpretation: a 56-day fill uses a card with double the blister count.\n        # If that exact size is not present in card_cost.csv, fall back to the medication's\n        # existing blister_card_count (data-driven conservative default).\n        blister_count_56 = blister_count_28 * 2\n        if blister_count_56 not in card_cost_by_count:\n            blister_count_56 = blister_count_28\n\n        if blister_count_28 not in card_cost_by_count:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"No card_cost_usd found for blister_card_count={blister_count_28} (medication={med}).\"\n            )\n        if blister_count_56 not in card_cost_by_count:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"No card_cost_usd found for blister_card_count={blister_count_56} (medication={med}).\"\n            )\n\n        card_cost_28 = card_cost_by_count[blister_count_28]\n        card_cost_56 = card_cost_by_count[blister_count_56]\n\n        reimbursement_per_cycle = reimbursement_by_med[med]\n\n        # Drug costs.\n        annual_drug_cost_28 = (price_per_1000 / Decimal(\"1000\")) * capsules_28 * fills_28 * patients\n        annual_drug_cost_56 = (price_per_1000 / Decimal(\"1000\")) * capsules_56 * fills_56 * patients\n\n        # Packaging costs.\n        annual_pack_cost_28 = card_cost_28 * patients * fills_28\n        annual_pack_cost_56 = card_cost_56 * patients * fills_56\n\n        # Reimbursement.\n        annual_reimb_28 = reimbursement_per_cycle * fills_28\n        annual_reimb_56 = reimbursement_per_cycle * fills_56\n\n        #
